# Content trace: Australia and the Allied Industrial Base

Each claim of the brief with the dashboard field it comes from, the source datasets and the evidence quality it rests on.

**s1.1** Australia's distinctive role in the Allied Industrial Base is as its trusted supplier of critical minerals: it holds the largest allied position in battery and EV minerals, and 51 economies have it as their best-placed trusted partner for battery minerals.

- `aib.recommended_by.batteries-extraction.n` = 51 | posture core cells.p1 (best-placed trusted partner of every economy) | Country strategy choices by technology and supply-chain step (supply-chain need, Strategic Industrial Value, Competitive Position, deployment need, policy alignment), computed by the Explorer's authors | evidence: AIB model run

**s1.9** The rare-earth chain from separation to magnets, where China holds 94% of world refining supply and trusted suppliers only 4%; the control software of grid equipment, inverters, batteries and turbines; and the control systems of gas and oil networks.

- `cells.electric-vehicles-refining.coercive_top` = China | posture detail cells[] (need components and trade) | United Nations, UN Comtrade Database, HS6 bilateral trade (all reporters); reconciled by the authors | evidence: position evidence strong; choice confidence moderate
- `cells.electric-vehicles-refining.coercive_top_share` = 94% | posture detail cells[] (need components and trade) | United Nations, UN Comtrade Database, HS6 bilateral trade (all reporters); reconciled by the authors | evidence: position evidence strong; choice confidence moderate
- `cells.electric-vehicles-refining.trusted_share` = 4% | posture detail cells[] (need components and trade) | United Nations, UN Comtrade Database, HS6 bilateral trade (all reporters); reconciled by the authors | evidence: position evidence strong; choice confidence moderate
